safe_eps(f):\n245\t        return max(abs(f), 1e-10)\n246\t\n247\t    for i in range(nx):\n248\t        for j in range(ny):\n249\t            dx_w = dx[i - 1] if i > 0 else dx[0]\n250\t            dx_e = dx[i] if i < nx - 1 else dx[-1]\n251\t            dy_s = dy[j - 1] if j > 0 else dy[0]\n252\t            dy_n = dy[j] if j < ny - 1 else dy[-1]\n253\t\n254\t            eps_xx, eps_xy, eps_yx,            # Get neighbor eps for averaging\n260\t            eps_xx_w = eps_xx\n261\t            eps_xx_e = eps_xx\n262\t            eps_yy_w = eps_yy\n263\t            eps_yy_e = eps_yy\n264\t            eps_xx_s = eps_xx\n265\t            eps_xx_n = eps_xx\n266\t            eps_yy_s = eps_yy\n267\t            eps_yy_n = eps_yy\n268\t\n269\t            if i > 0:\n270\t                eww, _, _, eyw, _ = epsilon(x[i - 1], y[j])\n271\t                eps_xx_w = safe_eps(eww)\n272\t                eps_yy_w = safe_eps(eyw)\n273\t            if i < nx - 1:\n274\t                ewe, _, _, eye, _ = epsilon(x[i + 1], y[j])\n275\t                eps_xx_e = safe_eps(ewe)\n276\t                eps_yy_e = safe_eps(eye)\n277\t            if j > 0:\n278\t                ews, _, _, eys, _ = epsilon(x[i], y[j - 1])\n279\t                eps_xx_s = safe_eps(ews)\n280\t                eps_yy_s = safe_eps(eys)\n281\t            if j < ny - 1:\n282\t                ewn, _, _, eyn, _ = epsilon(x[i], y[j + 1])\n283\t                eps_xx_n = safe_eps(ewn)\n284\t                eps_yy_n = safe_eps(eyn)\n285\t\n286\t            # === Simplified curl-curl discretization ===\n287\t            # For the 2D cross-section, the curl-curl operator gives:\n288\t            # ∇ × (μ⁻¹ ∇ × H) = β² H in the transverse plane\n289\t            # For μ = μ₀ (vacuum), we need:\n290\t            # [1/εxx d²/dx² + 1/εyy d²/dy²] Hx = -β² Hx  [along x]\n291\t            # [1/εyy d²/dy² + 1/εxx d²/dx²] Hy = -β² Hy  [along y]\n292\t            #\n293\t            # Rearranging: -[1/εxx d²/dx² + 1/εyy d²/dy²] Hx = β² Hx\n294\t            #\n295\t            # This gives a NEGATIVE-DEFINITE operator whose eigenvalues are β² > 0.\n296\t            #\n297\t            # The FD discretization uses harmonic averaging at cell interfaces:\n298\t            # 1/εxx at i+1/2 ≈ 2/(εxx_i + εxx_{i+1})\n299\t\n300\t            # Hx block (diagonal coefficient for Hx equation)\n301\t            # (1/εxx_e + 1/εxx_w)/(2*dx²) + (1/εyy_n + 1/εyy_s)/(2*dy²) - k₀²\n302\t            axxp = (\n303\t                (1.0 / eps_xx_e + 1.0 / eps_xx_w) / (2.0 * dx_e * dx_w)\n304\t                + (1.0 / eps_yy_n + 1.0 / eps_yy_s) / (2.0 * dy_n * dy_s)\n305\t                - k0**2\n306\t            )\n307\t\n308\t            # Hy block (diagonal coefficient for Hy equation)\n309\t            ayyp = (\n310\t                (1.0 / eps_yy_e + 1.0 / eps_yy_w) / (2.0 * dx_e * dx_w)\n311\t                + (1.0 / eps_xx_n + 1.0 / eps_xx_s) / (2.0 * dy_n * dy_s)\n312\t                - k0**2\n313\t            )\n314\t\n315\t            # Hx neighbors\n316\t            axxe = -(1.0 / eps_xx_e) / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e))  # West\n317\t            axxw = -(1.0 / eps_xx_w) / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e))  # East\n318\t            axxn = -(1.0 / eps_yy_n) / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n))  # South\n319\t            axxs = -(1.0 / eps_yy_s) / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n))  # North\n320\t\n321\t            # Hy neighbors\n322\t            ayye = -(1.0 / eps_yy_e) / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e))  # West\n323\t            ayyw = -(1.0 / eps_yy_w) / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e))  # East\n324\t            ayyn = -(1.0 / eps_xx_n) / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n))  # South\n325\t            ayys = -(1.0 / eps_xx_s) / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n))  # North\n326\t\n327\t            ix = i * ny + j\n328\t            iy = ix + N\n329\t\n330\t            # Apply BCs via direct modification of diagonal entries\n331\t            if j == ny - 1:\n332\t                if bc_north == 0:  # Dirichlet: Hx=0 at boundary\n333\t                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_north * axxs\n334\t                axxn += bc_north * axxs\n335\t                ayyp += bc_north * ayys\n336\t\n337\t            if j == 0:\n338\t                if bc_south == 0:\n339\t                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_south * axxn\n340\t                axxs += bc_south * axxn\n341\t                ayyp += bc_south * ayyn\n342\t\n343\t            if i == nx - 1:\n344\t                if bc_east == 0:\n345\t                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_east * axxe\n346\t                axxw += bc_east * axxe\n347\t                ayyp += bc_east * ayye\n348\t\n349\t            if i == 0:\n350\t                if bc_west == 0:\n351\t                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_west * axxw\n352\t                axxe += bc_west * axxw\n353\t                ayyp += bc_west * ayyw\n354\t\n355\t            # Assemble\n356\t            _add(ix, ix, axxp)\n357\t            _add(iy, iy, ayyp)\n358\t\n359\t            if j < ny - 1:\n360\t                _add(ix, i * ny + (j + 1), axxs)\n361\t                _add(iy, i * ny + (j + 1), ayys)\n362\t            if j > 0:\n363\t                _add(ix, i * ny + (j - 1), axxn)\n364\t                _add(iy, i * ny + (j - 1), A.eliminate_zeros()\n374\t    return A\n375\t\n376\t\n377\tdef _reconstruct_hz(\n378\t    Hx: np.ndarray, Hy: np.ndarray, x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, beta: float\n379\t) -> np.ndarray:\n380\t    \"\"\"Reconstruct Hz from Hx and Hy using Maxwell's curl equation.\n381\t\n382\t    Hx and Hy are on staggered grids; Hz is defined at cell centers.\n383\t    We compute Hz using the curl of H:\n384\t        Hz[k,l] = (dHx/dy - dHy/dx) / (i*beta) at cell center (k,l)\n385\t    \"\"\"\n386\t    ny, nx = Hx.shape\n387\t    # Hz is defined at interior cell centers (ny-1, nx-1)\n388\t    Hz = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n389\t\n390\t    diffx = np.diff(x)\n391\t    diffy = np.diff(y)\n392\t\n393\t    # Valid range: j from 0 to ny-2, i from 0 to nx-2\n394\t    for j in range(ny - 1):\n395\t        for i in range(nx - 1):\n396\t            dx = diffx[i] if i < len(diffx) else diffx[-1] if len(diffx) > 0 else 1.0\n397\t            dy = diffy[j] if j < len(diffy) else diffy[-1] if len(diffy) > 0 else 1.0\n398\t\n399\t            # Average H at face centers\n400\t            Hx_j_i = Hx[j, i] if j < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n401\t            Hx_j1_i = Hx[j + 1, i] if j + 1 < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n402\t            Hx_j_i1 = Hx[j, i + 1] if j < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n403\t            Hx_j1_i1 = Hx[j + 1, i + 1] if j + 1 < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n404\t\n405\t            Hy_j_i = Hy[j, i] if j < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n406\t            Hy_j1_i = Hy[j + 1, i] if j + 1 < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n407\t            Hy_j_i1 = Hy[j, i + 1] if j < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n408\t            Hy_j1_i1 = Hy[j + 1, i + 1] if j + 1 < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n409\t\n410\t            dHxd_y = (Hx_j1_i1 + Hx_j1_i - Hx_j_i1 - Hx_j_i) / (2.0 * dy)\n411\t            dHyd_x = (Hy_j1_i1 + Hy_j1_i - Hy_j_i1 - Hy_j_i) / (2.0 * dx)\n412\t            Hz[j, i] = (dHxd_y - dHyd_x) / (1j * beta + 1e-30)\n413\t\n414\t    return Hz\n415\t\n416\t\n417\tdef _reconstruct_fields(\n418\t    Hx: np.ndarray,\n419\t    Hy: np.ndarray,\n420\t    Hz: np.ndarray,\n421\t    x: np.ndarray,\n422\t    y: np.ndarray,\n423\t    beta: float,\n424\t    omega: float,\n425\t    epsilon: EpsilonCallback,\n426\t) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n427\t    \"\"\"Reconstruct E fields from H fields using Maxwell's curl equations.\"\"\"\n428\t    ny, nx = Hx.shape\n429\t    diffx = np.diff(x)\n430\t    diffy = np.diff(y)\n431\t\n432\t    Ex = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n433\t    Ey = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n434\t    Ez = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n435\t\n436\t    for j in range(ny - 1):\n437\t        for i in range(nx - 1):\n438\t            dx = diffx[i] if i < len(diffx) else diffx[-1] if len(diffx) > 0 else 1.0\n439\t            dy = diffy[j] if j < len(diffy) else diffy[-1] if len(diffy) > 0 else 1.0\n440\t            xc = (x[j] + x[j + 1]) / 2.0 if j < len(x) - 1 else x[j]\n441\t            yc = (y[i] + y[i + 1]) / 2.0 if i < len(y) - 1 else y[i]\n442\t\n443\t            eps_xx, eps_xy, eps_yx, eps_yy, eps_zz = epsilon(xc, yc)\n444\t            det_eps = eps_xx * eps_yy - eps_xy * eps_yx + 1e-15\n445\t\n446\t            # dHz/dy and dHz/dx using safe indexing\n447\t            def safe_hz(jj, ii):\n448\t                if 0 <= jj < Hz.shape[0] and 0 <= ii < Hz.shape[1]:\n449\t                    return Hz[jj, ii]\n450\t                return 0.0\n451\t\n452\t            dHz_dy = (safe_hz(j, i) + safe_hz(j + 1, i) - safe_hz(j, i + 1) - safe_hz(j + 1, i + 1)) / (2.0 * dy)\n453\t            dHz_dx = (safe_hz(j + 1, i + 1) + safe_hz(j + 1, i) - safe_hz(j, i + 1) - safe_hz(j, i)) / (2.0 * dx)\n454\t\n455\t            # Average H at cell center\n456\t            def safe_h(jj, ii, arr):\n457\t                if 0 <= jj < ny and 0 <= ii < nx:\n458\t                    return arr[jj, ii]\n459\t                return 0.0\n460\t\n461\t            Hy_avg = (safe_h(j + 1, i + 1, Hy) + safe_h(j + 1, i, Hy) + safe_h(j, i + 1, Hy) + safe_h(j, i, Hy)) / 4.0\n462\t            Hx_avg = (safe_h(j + 1, i + 1, Hx) + safe_h(j + 1, i, Hx) + safe_h(j, i + 1, Hx) + safe_h(j, i, Hx)) / 4.0\n463\t\n464\t            # E = (1/(-iωε)) curl H\n465\t            Dx = 1j * (dHz_dy - 1j * beta * Hy_avg) / (omega + 1e-30)\n466\t            Dy = 1j * (1j * beta * Hx_avg - dHz_dx) / (omega + 1e-30)\n467\t\n468\t            Ex[j, i] = (eps_yy * Dx - eps_yx * Dy) / det_eps\n469\t            Ey[j, i] = (-eps_xy * Dx + eps_xx * Dy) / det_eps\n470\t            Ez[j, i] = 0.0\n471\t\n472\t    return Ex, Ey, Ez\n473\t\n474\t\n475\tdef solve_modes(\n476\t    config: ModeSolverConfig,\n477\t    epsilon: EpsilonCallback,\n478\t    x: )\n528\t    except Exception as exc:\n529\t        raise RuntimeError(f\"eigensolver failed: {exc}\") from exc\n530\t\n531\t    modes: list[ModeSolution] = []\n532\t\n533\t    for idx in     if beta_sq.real >= 0:\n539\t            # Not a guided mode (would be radiating or evanescent)\n540\t            continue\n541\t\n542\t        beta_sq_neg = beta_sq.real  # eigenvalue of A power_norm),\n595\t                power=power * power_norm**2,\n596\t            )\n597\t        )\n598\t\n599\t        if len(modes) >= config.mode_spec.num_modes:\n600\t rectilinear\ncross-section.\n\nThe formulation solves for propagation modes in the z-direction, where\nthe cross-section lies in the x-y plane. The eigenvalue problem is:\n\n    ∇²ₜ Ez + k₀² εᵣ Ez = β² Ez   [TE polarization, E_z only]\n\nor equivalently the same eigenvalue problem for Hz (TM polarization).\n\nFor anisotropic media the full 6×6 curl-curl operator is used, but for\nisotropic or diagonal-anisotropic media a simplified scalar Helmholtz\napproach gives the correct dispersion relationship.\n\nThe propagation eigenvalue β² is solved via a sparse generalized eigenvalue\nproblem A · φ = β² · φ, where the matrix A encodes the transverse\nfinite-difference discretization of the wave operator.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import ModeSpec,\n)\n\n\n# Epsilon callback type: (x, y) -> (eps_xx, eps_xy, eps_yx, eps_yy, eps_zz)\nEpsilonCallback = Callable[[float, float], tuple[float, float, float, float, float]]\n\n\ndef _map_boundary_condition(bc: BoundaryCondition) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Map BoundaryCondition to integer code.\n\n    Returns:\n        0: Dirichlet (Ez=0 at boundary, PEC-like)\n        1: Neumann (dEz/dn=0, PMC-like)\n        2: Bloch periodic\n    \"\"\"\n    if bc.condition == \"dirichlet\":\n        return 0\n    if bc.condition == \"neumann\":\n        return 1\n    if bc.condition == \"bloch\":\n        return 2\n    raise ValueError(f\"unknown boundary condition: {bc.condition}\")\n\n\ndef assemble_te_mode_matrix(\n    x: tuple[float, ...],\n    y: tuple[float, ...],\n sparse.csr_matrix\":\n    \"\"\"Assemble the sparse FD matrix for TE modes (Ez-only Helmholtz equation).\n\n    Solves: ∇²Ez + k₀² εᵣ(x,y) Ez = β² Ez\n    discretized as:\n        (Ez_{i+1,j} - 2 dtype=np.float64)\n    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)\n    nx = len(x)\n    ny = len(y)\n    k0 = 2.0 * math.pi / wavelength\n\n    # Cell spacings\n    dx = np.diff(x) if nx > 1 else np.array([1.0])\n    dy = np.diff(y) if ny > 1 else np.array([1.0])\n\n    bc_north = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[0])\n    bc_south = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[1])\n    bc_east = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[2])\n    bc_west = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[3])\n\n    rows: list[int] = []\n    cols: list[int] = []\n    data: list[float] = []\n\n    def _add(ii: int, jj: int, val: float) -> None:\n        if abs(val) > 1e-15:\n            rows.append(ii)\n            cols.append(jj)\n            data.append(val)\n\n    for i in range(nx):\n        for j in range(ny):\n            # Cell dimensions\n            dx_w = dx[i - 1] if i > 0 else dx[0]\n            dx_e = dx[i] if i < nx - 1 else dx[-1]\n            dy_s = dy[j - 1] if j > 0 else dy[0]\n            dy_n = dy[j] if j < ny - 1 else dy[-1]\n\n            # ε at cell center\n            eps_c = max(epsilon_zz(x[i], y[j]), 1e-10)\n\n            # Standard 5-point Laplacian stencil\n            # (Ez_{i+1,j} - 2Ez_{i,j} + Ez_{i-1,j}) / dx²\n            # + (Ez_{i,j+1} - 2Ez_{i,j} + Ez_{i,j-1}) / dy²\n            # + k₀² ε_r(i,j) Ez_{i,j}\n\n            # Diagonal\n            diag = (\n                -2.0 / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e))\n                - 2.0 / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e))\n                - 2.0 / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n))\n                - 2.0 / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n))\n                + k0**2 * eps_c\n            )\n\n            idx = i * ny + j\n\n            # Apply BCs via ghost points or direct modification\n            # Dirichlet (PEC): Ez=0 at boundary\n            # Neumann (PMC): dEz/dn=0\n\n            # For simplicity, we implement BCs by modifying the diagonal\n            # and neighbor coefficients at the boundary\n\n            if i == 0:  # West\n                # Ez at ghost point i=-1: Neumann: Ez_{-1} = Ez_0\n                # Dirichlet: Ez_{-1} = -Ez_0 (to force Ez=0 at wall)\n                if bc_west == 0:  # Dirichlet\n                    diag += 2.0 / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e))\n                # Neumann: no change (ghost = same as interior)\n\n            if i == nx - 1:  # East\n                if bc_east == 0:  # Dirichlet\n                    diag += 2.0 / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e))\n\n            if j == 0:  # South\n                if bc_south == 0:  # Dirichlet\n                    diag += 2.0 / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n))\n\n            if j == ny - 1:  # North\n                if bc_north == 0:  # Dirichlet\n                    diag += 2.0 / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n))\n\n            _add(idx, idx, diag)\n\n            # Neighbor contributions\n            if i > 0:\n                _add(idx, (i - 1) * ny + j, 2.0 / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e)))\n            if i < nx - 1:\n                _add(idx, (i + 1) * ny + j, 2.0 / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e)))\n            if j > 0:\n                _add(idx, i * ny + (j - 1), 2.0 / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n)))\n            if j < ny - 1:\n                _add(idx, i * ny + (j + 1), 2.0 / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n)))\n\n    A = sparse.csc_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(nx * ny, nx * ny), dtype=np.float64)\n    A.eliminate_zeros()\n    return A\n\n\ndef assemble_mode_matrix(\n    x: tuple[float, ...],\n    y: tuple[float, ...],\n    wavelength: float,\n    epsilon: EpsilonCallback,\n    boundaries: tuple[BoundaryCondition, BoundaryCondition, BoundaryCondition, BoundaryCondition],\n) -> \"sparse.csc_matrix | sparse.csr_matrix\":\n    \"\"\"Assemble the sparse FD matrix for the vector mode problem.\n\n    For the general anisotropic case, we solve the full 2-component\n    vector eigenvalue problem using the Yee-grid curl-curl operator.\n\n    Args:\n        x: Cell-center x coordinates\n        y: Cell-center y coordinates\n        wavelength: Free-space wavelength\n        epsilon: Callback returning (eps_xx, eps_xy, eps_yx, eps_yy, eps_zz)\n        boundaries: [north, south, east, west] BCs\n\n    Returns:\n        Sparse matrix A of shape (2*N, 2*N) for A·[Hx;Hy] = β²·[Hx;Hy]\n    \"\"\"\n    if sparse is None:\n        raise ModuleNotFoundError(\"scipy is required for the mode solver\")\n\n    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)\n    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)\n    nx = len(x)\n    ny = len(y)\n    k0 = 2.0 * math.pi / wavelength\n\n    dx = np.diff(x) if nx > 1 else np.array([1.0])\n    dy = np.diff(y) if ny > 1 else np.array([1.0])\n\n    bc_north = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[0])\n    bc_south = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[1])\n    bc_east = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[2])\n    bc_west = _map_boundary_condition(boundaries[3])\n\n    N = nx * ny\n    rows: list[int] = []\n    cols: list[int] = []\n    data: list[float] = []\n\n    def _add(ii: int, jj: int, val: float) -> None:\n        if abs(val) > 1e-15:\n            rows.append(ii)\n            cols.append(jj)\n            data.append(val)\n\n    def safe_eps(f):\n        return max(abs(f), 1e-10)\n\n    for i in range(nx):\n        for j in range(ny):\n            dx_w = dx[i - 1] if i > 0 else dx[0]\n            dx_e = dx[i] if i < nx - 1 else dx[-1]\n            dy_s = dy[j - 1] if j > 0 else dy[0]\n            dy_n = dy[j] if j < ny - 1 else dy[-1]\n\n            eps_xx, eps_xy, eps_yx, eps_yy, eps_zz = epsilon(x[i], y[j])\n            eps_xx = safe_eps(eps_xx)\n            eps_yy = safe_eps(eps_yy)\n            det_eps = eps_xx * eps_yy - eps_xy * eps_yx\n\n            # Get neighbor eps for averaging\n            eps_xx_w = eps_xx\n            eps_xx_e = eps_xx\n            eps_yy_w = eps_yy\n            eps_yy_e = eps_yy\n            eps_xx_s = eps_xx\n            eps_xx_n = eps_xx\n            eps_yy_s = eps_yy\n            eps_yy_n = eps_yy\n\n            if i > 0:\n                eww, _, _, eyw, _ = epsilon(x[i - 1], y[j])\n                eps_xx_w = safe_eps(eww)\n                eps_yy_w = safe_eps(eyw)\n            if i < nx - 1:\n                ewe, _, _, eye, _ = epsilon(x[i + 1], y[j])\n                eps_xx_e = safe_eps(ewe)\n                eps_yy_e = safe_eps(eye)\n            if j > 0:\n                ews, _, _, eys, _ = epsilon(x[i], y[j - 1])\n                eps_xx_s = safe_eps(ews)\n                eps_yy_s = safe_eps(eys)\n            if j < ny - 1:\n                ewn, _, _, eyn, _ = epsilon(x[i], y[j + 1])\n                eps_xx_n = safe_eps(ewn)\n                eps_yy_n = safe_eps(eyn)\n\n            # === Simplified curl-curl discretization ===\n            # For the 2D cross-section, the curl-curl operator gives:\n            # ∇ × (μ⁻¹ ∇ × H) = β² H in the transverse plane\n            # For μ = μ₀ (vacuum), we need:\n            # [1/εxx d²/dx² + 1/εyy d²/dy²] Hx = -β² Hx  [along x]\n            # [1/εyy d²/dy² + 1/εxx d²/dx²] Hy = -β² Hy  [along y]\n            #\n            # Rearranging: -[1/εxx d²/dx² + 1/εyy d²/dy²] Hx = β² Hx\n            #\n            # This gives a NEGATIVE-DEFINITE operator whose eigenvalues are β² > 0.\n            #\n            # The FD discretization uses harmonic averaging at cell interfaces:\n            # 1/εxx at i+1/2 ≈ 2/(εxx_i + εxx_{i+1})\n\n            # Hx block (diagonal coefficient for Hx equation)\n            # (1/εxx_e + 1/εxx_w)/(2*dx²) + (1/εyy_n + 1/εyy_s)/(2*dy²) - k₀²\n            axxp = (\n                (1.0 / eps_xx_e + 1.0 / eps_xx_w) / (2.0 * dx_e * dx_w)\n                + (1.0 / eps_yy_n + 1.0 / eps_yy_s) / (2.0 * dy_n * dy_s)\n                - k0**2\n            )\n\n            # Hy block (diagonal coefficient for Hy equation)\n            ayyp = (\n                (1.0 / eps_yy_e + 1.0 / eps_yy_w) / (2.0 * dx_e * dx_w)\n                + (1.0 / eps_xx_n + 1.0 / eps_xx_s) / (2.0 * dy_n * dy_s)\n                - k0**2\n            )\n\n            # Hx neighbors\n            axxe = -(1.0 / eps_xx_e) / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e))  # West\n            axxw = -(1.0 / eps_xx_w) / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e))  # East\n            axxn = -(1.0 / eps_yy_n) / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n))  # South\n            axxs = -(1.0 / eps_yy_s) / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n))  # North\n\n            # Hy neighbors\n            ayye = -(1.0 / eps_yy_e) / (dx_e * (dx_w + dx_e))  # West\n            ayyw = -(1.0 / eps_yy_w) / (dx_w * (dx_w + dx_e))  # East\n            ayyn = -(1.0 / eps_xx_n) / (dy_n * (dy_s + dy_n))  # South\n            ayys = -(1.0 / eps_xx_s) / (dy_s * (dy_s + dy_n))  # North\n\n            ix = i * ny + j\n            iy = ix + N\n\n            # Apply BCs via direct modification of diagonal entries\n            if j == ny - 1:\n                if bc_north == 0:  # Dirichlet: Hx=0 at boundary\n                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_north * axxs\n                axxn += bc_north * axxs\n                ayyp += bc_north * ayys\n\n            if j == 0:\n                if bc_south == 0:\n                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_south * axxn\n                axxs += bc_south * axxn\n                ayyp += bc_south * ayyn\n\n            if i == nx - 1:\n                if bc_east == 0:\n                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_east * axxe\n                axxw += bc_east * axxe\n                ayyp += bc_east * ayye\n\n            if i == 0:\n                if bc_west == 0:\n                    axxp += 2.0 * bc_west * axxw\n                axxe += bc_west * axxw\n                ayyp += bc_west * ayyw\n\n            # Assemble\n            _add(ix, ix, axxp)\n            _add(iy, iy, ayyp)\n\n            if j < ny - 1:\n                _add(ix, i * ny + (j + 1), axxs)\n                _add(iy, i * ny + (j + 1), ayys)\n            if j > 0:\n                _add(ix, i * ny + (j - 1), axxn)\n                _add(iy, i * ny + (j - 1), ayyn)\n            if i < nx - 1:\n                _add(ix, (i + 1) * ny + j, axxe)\n                _add(iy, (i + 1) * ny + j, ayye)\n            if i > 0:\n                _add(ix, (i - 1) * ny + j, axxw)\n                _add(iy, (i - 1) * ny + j, ayyw)\n\n    A = sparse.csc_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * N, 2 * N), dtype=np.float64)\n    A.eliminate_zeros()\n    return A\n\n\ndef _reconstruct_hz(\n    Hx: np.ndarray, Hy: np.ndarray, x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, beta: float\n) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Reconstruct Hz from Hx and Hy using Maxwell's curl equation.\n\n    Hx and Hy are on staggered grids; Hz is defined at cell centers.\n    We compute Hz using the curl of H:\n        Hz[k,l] = (dHx/dy - dHy/dx) / (i*beta) at cell center (k,l)\n    \"\"\"\n    ny, nx = Hx.shape\n    # Hz is defined at interior cell centers (ny-1, nx-1)\n    Hz = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n\n    diffx = np.diff(x)\n    diffy = np.diff(y)\n\n    # Valid range: j from 0 to ny-2, i from 0 to nx-2\n    for j in range(ny - 1):\n        for i in range(nx - 1):\n            dx = diffx[i] if i < len(diffx) else diffx[-1] if len(diffx) > 0 else 1.0\n            dy = diffy[j] if j < len(diffy) else diffy[-1] if len(diffy) > 0 else 1.0\n\n            # Average H at face centers\n            Hx_j_i = Hx[j, i] if j < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n            Hx_j1_i = Hx[j + 1, i] if j + 1 < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n            Hx_j_i1 = Hx[j, i + 1] if j < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n            Hx_j1_i1 = Hx[j + 1, i + 1] if j + 1 < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n\n            Hy_j_i = Hy[j, i] if j < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n            Hy_j1_i = Hy[j + 1, i] if j + 1 < ny and i < nx else 0.0\n            Hy_j_i1 = Hy[j, i + 1] if j < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n            Hy_j1_i1 = Hy[j + 1, i + 1] if j + 1 < ny and i + 1 < nx else 0.0\n\n            dHxd_y = (Hx_j1_i1 + Hx_j1_i - Hx_j_i1 - Hx_j_i) / (2.0 * dy)\n            dHyd_x = (Hy_j1_i1 + Hy_j1_i - Hy_j_i1 - Hy_j_i) / (2.0 * dx)\n            Hz[j, i] = (dHxd_y - dHyd_x) / (1j * beta + 1e-30)\n\n    return Hz\n\n\ndef _reconstruct_fields(\n    Hx: np.ndarray,\n    Hy: np.ndarray,\n    Hz: np.ndarray,\n    x: np.ndarray,\n    y: np.ndarray,\n    beta: float,\n    omega: float,\n    epsilon: EpsilonCallback,\n) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Reconstruct E fields from H fields using Maxwell's curl equations.\"\"\"\n    ny, nx = Hx.shape\n    diffx = np.diff(x)\n    diffy = np.diff(y)\n\n    Ex = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n    Ey = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n    Ez = np.zeros((ny - 1, nx - 1), dtype=np.complex128)\n\n    for j in range(ny - 1):\n        for i in range(nx - 1):\n            dx = diffx[i] if i < len(diffx) else diffx[-1] if len(diffx) > 0 else 1.0\n            dy = diffy[j] if j < len(diffy) else diffy[-1] if len(diffy) > 0 else 1.0\n            xc = (x[j] + x[j + 1]) / 2.0 if j < len(x) - 1 else x[j]\n            yc = (y[i] + y[i + 1]) / 2.0 if i < len(y) - 1 else y[i]\n\n            eps_xx, eps_xy, eps_yx, eps_yy, eps_zz = epsilon(xc, yc)\n            det_eps = eps_xx * eps_yy - eps_xy * eps_yx + 1e-15\n\n            # dHz/dy and dHz/dx using safe indexing\n            def safe_hz(jj, ii):\n                if 0 <= jj < Hz.shape[0] and 0 <= ii < Hz.shape[1]:\n                    return Hz[jj, ii]\n                return 0.0\n\n            dHz_dy = (safe_hz(j, i) + safe_hz(j + 1, i) - safe_hz(j, i + 1) - safe_hz(j + 1, i + 1)) / (2.0 * dy)\n            dHz_dx = (safe_hz(j + 1, i + 1) + safe_hz(j + 1, i) - safe_hz(j, i + 1) - safe_hz(j, i)) / (2.0 * dx)\n\n            # Average H at cell center\n            def safe_h(jj, ii, arr):\n                if 0 <= jj < ny and 0 <= ii < nx:\n                    return arr[jj, ii]\n                return 0.0\n\n            Hy_avg = (safe_h(j + 1, i + 1, Hy) + safe_h(j + 1, i, Hy) + safe_h(j, i + 1, Hy) + safe_h(j, i, Hy)) / 4.0\n            Hx_avg = (safe_h(j + 1, i + 1, Hx) + safe_h(j + 1, i, Hx) + safe_h(j, i + 1, Hx) + safe_h(j, i, Hx)) / 4.0\n\n            # E = (1/(-iωε)) curl H\n            Dx = 1j * (dHz_dy - 1j * beta * Hy_avg) / (omega + 1e-30)\n            Dy = 1j * (1j * beta * Hx_avg - dHz_dx) / (omega + 1e-30)\n\n            Ex[j, i] = (eps_yy * Dx - eps_yx * Dy) / det_eps\n            Ey[j, i] = (-eps_xy * Dx + eps_xx * Dy) / det_eps\n            Ez[j, i] = 0.0\n\n    return Ex, Ey, Ez\n\n\ndef solve_modes(\n    config: ModeSolverConfig,\n    epsilon: EpsilonCallback,\n    x: tuple[float, ...],\n    y: tuple[float, ...],\n) -> tuple[ModeSolution, ...]:\n    \"\"\"Solve for waveguide modes on a cross-sectional grid.\n\n    Args:\n        config: Mode solver configuration\n        epsilon: Callback returning permittivity tensor at (x, y)\n        x: Cell-center x coordinates\n        y: Cell-center y coordinates\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of ModeSolution objects sorted by neff descending\n    \"\"\"\n    if eigs is None:\n        raise ModuleNotFoundError(\"scipy is required for the mode solver\")\n\n    nx = len(x)\n    ny = len(y)\n    N = nx * ny\n\n    if N < 4:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Grid too small: {nx} x {ny} cells; need at least 2x2\")\n\n    omega = 2.0 * math.pi / config.wavelength\n    k = omega  # k₀ in the convention used\n\n    # Assemble sparse matrix\n    A = assemble_mode_matrix(x, y, config.wavelength, epsilon, config.cross_section.boundaries)\n\n    # Target number of eigenvalues\n    nev = min(config.mode_spec.num_modes, max(1, N // 4))\n\n    # Eigensolver sigma: target neff or None for largest real part\n    sigma = None\n    if config.mode_spec.target_neff is not None:\n        sigma = config.mode_spec.target_neff**2 * k**2  # β² = neff² * k²\n\n    # The curl-curl operator is negative definite\n    # So A·H = -β²·H, giving eigenvalues -β² < 0\n    # We want the LARGEST algebraic eigenvalue (closest to zero)\n    try:\n        evals, evecs = eigs(\n            A,\n            k=nev + 5,  # Request extra to handle filtering\n            sigma=sigma,\n            tol=config.tolerance,\n            which=\"LM\" if sigma is not None else \"SR\",  # Smallest Real when no sigma\n            maxiter=2000,\n        )\n    except Exception as exc:\n        raise RuntimeError(f\"eigensolver failed: {exc}\") from exc\n\n    modes: list[ModeSolution] = []\n\n    for idx in range(len(evals)):\n        beta_sq = evals[idx]\n\n        # curl-curl gives -β², so β² = -eigenvalue\n        # The eigenvalues should be negative for propagating modes\n        if beta_sq.real >= 0:\n            # Not a guided mode (would be radiating or evanescent)\n            continue\n\n        beta_sq_neg = beta_sq.real  # eigenvalue of A (negative = -β²)\n        beta_sq_positive = -beta_sq_neg  # this is β² > 0\n\n        if beta_sq_positive <= 0:\n            continue\n\n        beta = math.sqrt(beta_sq_positive)\n        neff = beta / k if k > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Skip if neff is not in physical range\n        if neff < 0.0 or neff > 10.0:\n            continue\n\n        # Extract Hx, Hy\n        Hx_flat = evecs[:N, idx]\n        Hy_flat = evecs[N : 2 * N, idx]\n\n        Hx = Hx_flat.reshape((ny, nx))\n        Hy = Hy_flat.reshape((ny, nx))\n\n        # Reconstruct Hz\n        Hz = _reconstruct_hz(Hx, Hy, np.asarray(x), np.asarray(y), beta)\n\n        # Reconstruct E fields\n        Ex, Ey, Ez = _reconstruct_fields(Hx, Hy, Hz, np.asarray(x), np.asarray(y), beta, omega, epsilon)\n\n        # Compute power normalization\n        power = float(\n            np.sum(np.abs(Ex) ** 2) + np.sum(np.abs(Ey) ** 2) + np.sum(np.abs(Ez) ** 2)\n        )\n        power_norm = 1.0 / math.sqrt(power) if power > 1e-15 else 1.0\n\n        def _to_tuple_complex(arr: np.ndarray) -> tuple[tuple[float, float], ...]:\n            flat = arr.flatten()\n            return tuple((float(v.real), float(v.imag)) for v in flat)\n\n        # Downsample H to cell centers\n        Hx_c = (Hx[:-1, :-1] + Hx[1:, :-1] + Hx[:-1, 1:] + Hx[1:, 1:]) / 4.0\n        Hy_c = (Hy[:-1, :-1] + Hy[1:, :-1] + Hy[:-1, 1:] + Hy[1:, 1:]) / 4.0\n        Hz_c = Hz\n\n        modes.append(\n            ModeSolution(\n                neff=neff,\n                wavelength=config.wavelength,\n                x=x,\n                y=y,\n                Ex=_to_tuple_complex(Ex * power_norm),\n                Ey=_to_tuple_complex(Ey * power_norm),\n                Ez=_to_tuple_complex(Ez * power_norm),\n                Hx=_to_tuple_complex(Hx_c * power_norm),\n                Hy=_to_tuple_complex(Hy_c * power_norm),\n                Hz=_to_tuple_complex(Hz_c * power_norm),\n                power=power * power_norm**2,\n            )\n        )\n\n        if len(modes) >= config.mode_spec.num_modes:\n            break\n\n    # Sort by neff descending\n    modes.sort(key=lambda m: -m.neff_real)\n    return tuple(modes)\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"EpsilonCallback\",\n accumulation in a frequency-domain monitor.\n\n    For FieldMonitor with frequency points, this accumulates the DFT sum:\n    F(omega) = sum_n f(t_n) * exp(-i * omega * t_n)\n\n    Args:\n        electric_field: The E field buffer with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n        magnetic_field: The H field buffer with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n        monitor_state: FieldMonitorState with compiled placement electric_field, magnetic_field, field, compiled.placements\n        )\n        if monitor_state.dft_data is not None and field in monitor_state.dft_data:\n            omega_t = 2.0 * np.pi * placements: tuple[tuple[int, int, int], ...],\n) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute flux through a surface.\n\n    The flux is computed as the integral of H × E over the surface,\n    with the direction determining the sign convention.\n\n    Args:\n        electric_field: The E field buffer with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n        magnetic_field: The H field buffer with shape (nx, ny, nz, 3)\n        direction: Flux direction (+ or -)\n        axis: The normal axis for the flux surface\n        placements: Surface cell indices\n\n    Returns:\n        Total flux value\n    \"\"\"\n    sign = 1.0 the axis:\n    # - x-axis: S_x = E_y * H_z - E_z * H_y\n    # - y-axis: S_y = E_z * H_x - E_x * H_z\n    # - z-axis: S_z = E_x * H_y - E_y * H_x\n\n    tang_axes = tuple(a for a in range(3) if a != axis)\n\n    flux = 0.0\n    for idx in placements:\n        ex = electric_field[idx][0]\n        ey = electric_field[idx][1]\n        ez = electric_field[idx][2]\n        hx = magnetic_field[idx][0]\n        hy = magnetic_field[idx][1]\n        hz = magnetic_field[idx][2]\n\n        if axis == 0:\n            s_normal = ey * hz - ez * hy\n        elif axis == 1:\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_compute_mode_overlap_simple() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Compute overlap with a simple uniform mode.\"\"\"\n    x_coords = tuple(float(i) for i in range(3))\n    y_coords = tuple(float(i) for i in range(3))\n    n_pts = len(x_coords) * len(y_coords)\n\n    # Create a simple uniform mode\n    mode_solution = ModeSolution(\n        neff=1.5,\n        wavelength=1e-6,\n        x=x_coords,\n        y=y_coords,\n        Ex=tuple((1.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Ey=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Ez=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Hx=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Hy=tuple((0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Hz=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        power=1.0,\n    )\n\n    # Create uniform field data matching the mode\n    n_pts = len(x_coords) * len(y_coords)\n    field_components = {\n        \"Ex\": np.array([1.0 + 0.0j] * n_pts),\n        \"Ey\": np.array([0.0 + 0.0j] * n_pts),\n        \"Ez\": np.array([0.0 + 0.0j] * n_pts),\n        \"Hx\": np.array([0.0 + 0.0j] * n_pts),\n        \"Hy\": np.array([0.0 + 1.0j] * n_pts),\n        \"Hz\": np.array([0.0 + 0.0j] * n_pts),\n    }\n\n    placements = tuple((i, j, 0) for i in range(3) for j in range(3))\n\n    overlap = compute_mode_overlap(field_components, mode_solution, 2, placements)\n\n    # Should have non-zero overlap for matching fields\n    assert isinstance(overlap, complex)\n\n\ndef test_compute_mode_overlap_zero_fields() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Compute overlap with zero fields gives zero result.\"\"\"\n    x_coords = tuple(float(i) for i in range(3))\n    y_coords = tuple(float(i) Ex=tuple((1.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Ey=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Ez=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Hx=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Hy=tuple((0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        Hz=tuple((0.0, 0.0) for _ in range(n_pts)),\n        power=1.0,\n    )\n\n    # All zero fields\n    field_components = {\n
